# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn in the Patriarchal Church (Opening)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τοῦ Ἀκαθίστου Ὕμνου

Η  
ΕΝ ΤΩ ΠΑΤΡΙΑΡΧΙΚΩ ΝΑΩ  
ΤΑΞΙΣ ΤΗΣ ΑΚΟΛΟΥΘΙΑΣ  
ΤΟΥ ΑΚΑΘΙΣΤΟΥ ΥΜΝΟΥ  

---

Τῇ Παρασκευῇ περὶ τὴν θ΄ ὥραν, κατελθὼν ὁ Πατριάρχης ἐν τῷ Ναῷ μετὰ τῆς συνήθους παρατάξεως, ἐν τῷ Νάρθηκι ἐνδύεται τὸν Μανδύαν, καὶ φέρων τὴν Ποιμαντορικὴν ῥάβδον (πατερίτζαν) εἰσέρχεται ἐν τῷ Ναῷ, καὶ εὐλογήσας τὸν λαὸν ἀνέρχεται εἰς τὸν Θρόνον. Ψαλλομένου δὲ τοῦ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη ὑπὸ τῶν Χορῶν, λαμβάνουσι καιρὸν ὅ τε Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης μετὰ τοῦ Μ. Ἀρχιδιακόνου καὶ τοῦ Δευτερεύοντος, καὶ μετ’ αὐτοὺς ὁ Τριτεύων μεθ’ ἑνὸς ἑτέρου Διακόνου. Καὶ ὁ μὲν Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τοῦ Δευτερεύοντος ἵστανται παρὰ τῷ Θρόνῳ, οἱ δὲ εἰσέρχονται ἐν τῷ Ἱερῷ. Ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης ἐκφωνεῖ Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν· ὁ Πατριάρχης ἄρχεται Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι . . . Βασιλεῦ οὐράνιε, Παράκλητε κτλ., ὁ δὲ ταχθεὶς ἀναγνώστης Ἱερεὺς ἱστάμενος ἀπέναντι τοῦ Πατριάρχου παρὰ τῷ Πριμικηρίῳ, λέγει τὸ Τρισάγιον καὶ τὸ μικρὸν Ἀπόδειπνον. Μετὰ τὸ Ἄξιόν ἐστιν, ἄρχεται ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ψάλλων Τὸ προσταχθὲν μυστικῶς, ἀργῶς καὶ μετὰ μέλους· τούτου ψαλλομένου, οἱ μέλλοντες χοροστατῆσαι Ἀρχιερεῖς ἐξέρχονται ἐκ τοῦ Ἱεροῦ Βήματος διὰ τῆς νοτίου τοῦ Ἱεροῦ Πύλης, προπορευομένου τοῦ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχου καὶ ἵστανται ἕκαστος εἰς τὴν οἰκείαν θέσιν· ὁ δὲ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχης

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn in the Patriarchal Church (Opening)

## The Order of Service of the Akathist Hymn in the Patriarchal Church

# THE ORDER OF SERVICE OF THE AKATHIST HYMN IN THE PATRIARCHAL CHURCH

---

On Friday, about the Ninth Hour [3:00 p.m.], the Patriarch, having come down to the Church with the customary entourage, is vested with the Mandyas [Mantle] in the Narthex. Carrying the Pastoral Staff (*Pateritsa*), he enters the Nave, and, having blessed the people, ascends his Throne. While **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*) is sung by the Choirs, the Great Archimandrite, the Great Archdeacon, and the Second Deacon receive Keron [the liturgical blessing and permission to officiate]* and, after them, so do the Third Deacon and another Deacon. The Great Archdeacon and the Second Deacon stand by the Patriarchal Throne, while the other clergymen enter into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema]. The Great Archimandrite intones the Exclamation: **"Blessed is our God always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*); the Patriarch begins: **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee!"**, **"O Heavenly King, Comforter, Spirit of truth..."** (*Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι... Βασιλεῦ οὐράνιε, Παράκλητε...*), and the designated Reader-Priest, standing opposite the Patriarch near the Primikirios [Leader of Processions and participant in the chanting of the chanters],** reads the Trisagion and Small Compline. After **"It is truly right"** (*Ἄξιόν ἐστιν*), the First Choir begins chanting the Troparion in Tone 8: **"Taking knowledge of the secret command"** (*Τὸ προσταχθὲν μυστικῶς*), slowly and melodiously. While this hymn is being sung, the Hierarchs who are about to officiate exit the Holy Sanctuary by its South Gate, preceded by the Great Ecclesiarch, and stand each in his proper place [designated by seniority].

---

* *Note on Keron:* A clergyman receives the permission and blessing of the highest-ranking officiating clergyman (in this case the Patriarch) to participate in the service at hand.  
** *Note on Court Offices:* Offices (*offikia*) such as the Primikirios and the Megas Logothetis involved certain ecclesiastical, political, and administrative duties during the Byzantine Empire. In post-Ottoman times, these offices and titles became largely ceremonial. (See Iakovos Pilidis, *Titli, Offikia ke Axiomata en ti Vyzantini Aftokratoria ke ti Christianiki Orthodoxo Ekklisia*, Athens, 1985; also *Mega Efchologion*, Venice, 1862; repr. Athens, 1986, pp. 686–691)...[^487]

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (First Stasis and Canon Odes 1 and 3)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τοῦ Ἀκαθίστου Ὕμνου (Συνέχεια)

ποιήσας σχῆμα τῷ Πατριάρχῃ, ἐπανέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερὸν Βῆμα, ὡς καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τοῦ Δευτερεύοντος· ἀρχομένου δὲ καὶ τοῦ β΄ Χοροῦ ψάλλειν τὸ αὐτὸ μέλος, ἐξέρχονται οἱ Διάκονοι προπορευομένου τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου, τῆς Ὡραίας πύλης ἐνδεδυμένοι τὰς στολὰς αὑτῶν καὶ κρατοῦντες δικηροτρίκηρα ἵστανται παρὰ τῷ Θρόνῳ τοῦ Πατριάρχου, ἐκ δεξιῶν μὲν ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τοῦ Τριτεύοντος, ἐξ ἀριστερῶν δὲ ὁ Δευτερεύων μετὰ τοῦ ἑτέρου. Μεσούντος τοῦ μέλους τοῦ β΄ Χοροῦ, ὁ Πατριάρχης κατέρχεται τοῦ Θρόνου, καὶ προπορευομένου τοῦ τε Πριμικηρίου καὶ τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου καὶ τῶν Διακόνων, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν· καὶ ὁ μὲν Πριμικήριος μένει ἱστάμενος ἔμπροσθεν τῆς ἁγίας Εἰκόνος τῆς Θεοτόκου, ὁ δὲ Πατριάρχης ἀσπασάμενος τὴν ἁγίαν Τράπεζαν, ἐνδύεται παρὰ τοῦ Μ. Ἀρχιδιακόνου τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ τὸ Ὠμοφόριον· ἐνῷ δὲ ψάλλεται ὑπὸ τοῦ α΄ Χοροῦ τὸ τρίτον Τὸ προσταχθὲν ἀργοσυντόμως, ὁ Πατριάρχης ἐξέρχεται τοῦ Ἱεροῦ Βήματος προπορευομένων αὖθις τῶν Διακόνων, καὶ στὰς ἐνώπιον τῆς ἁγίας Εἰκόνος καὶ θυμιάσας τρὶς, ἄρχεται ἀναγινώσκειν τὴν α΄ Στάσιν τῶν Οἴκων θυμιῶν ἀνὰ τρὶς εἰς τὸ τέλος ἑκάστου Οἴκου· πληρωθείσης τῆς α΄ τῶν Οἴκων Στάσεως, ἀσπάζεται τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα καὶ στρέψας εὐλογεῖ τὸν λαὸν μετὰ τρικηρίου, τῶν Χορῶν ψαλλόντων τὸ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἅγιον Βῆμα, καὶ ἀποθέσας τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ τὸ Ὠμοφόριον, ἐξέρχεται προπορευομένων τοῦ τε Πριμικηρίου καὶ τῶν δύο Διακόνων καὶ ἀνέρχεται εἰς τὸν Θρόνον· ὁ δὲ Πριμικήριος ποιήσας σχῆμα ἀποχωρεῖ, καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος, ὡς καὶ ὁ Δευτερεύων, ἀποθέμενοι τὰς ἱερατικὰς στολὰς καὶ τὰ δικηροτρίκηρα ἐξέρχονται καὶ ἵστανται παρὰ τῷ Θρόνῳ· οἱ Χοροὶ ἐν τῷ μεταξὺ τούτῳ ψάλλουσι τὴν α΄ καὶ γ΄ Ὠδὴν τοῦ Κανόνος καὶ μετὰ τὸ τέλος ψάλλεται τὸ Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ ἀργῶς καὶ μετὰ μέλους ὑπὸ τῶν δύο χορῶν· κατὰ δὲ τὸν πρωτελευταῖον Στίχον τούτου ὁ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχης προσελθὼν τῷ α΄ τῶν χοροστατούντων Ἀρχιερεῖ διὰ σχήματος προσκαλεῖ αὐτὸν καὶ οὗτος ἀσπασάμενος τὴν χεῖρα τοῦ Πατριάρχου εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἅγιον Βῆμα, προπορευομένου τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου, καὶ ἐνδύεται Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ Ὠμοφόριον,

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (First Stasis and Canon Odes 1 and 3)

## Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (First Stasis and Canon Odes 1 and 3)

The Great Ecclesiarch, making a sign*** to the Patriarch, returns to the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], as do also the Great Archdeacon along with the Second Deacon. As the Second Choir begins chanting the same hymn: **"Taking knowledge of the secret command"** (*Τὸ προσταχθὲν μυστικῶς*), the Deacons, dressed in their vestments, holding Dikerotrikera [two candelabras: one with three candles, and the other with two]****, and led by the Ecclesiarch, exit the Holy Sanctuary through the Beautiful Gate. They come and stand by the Throne of the Patriarch: on the right, the Great Archdeacon with the Third Deacon; on the left, the Second Deacon with another Deacon. At the midpoint of the hymn: **"Taking knowledge of the secret command..."**, sung now by the Second Choir, the Patriarch descends from his Throne and, preceded by the Primikirios, the Ecclesiarch, and the Deacons, enters the Holy Sanctuary. The Primikirios, however, remains outside on the Soleas, standing before the Holy Icon of the Theotokos. The Patriarch, in the meantime, having kissed the Holy Table, is vested with the Epitrachilion and Omophorion by the Great Archdeacon. While the First Choir repeats for the third time the hymn: **"Taking knowledge of the secret command..."** at a moderate tempo, the Patriarch, preceded again by the Deacons, exits the Holy Sanctuary. Standing in front of the Holy Icon and having censed it three times, the Patriarch begins reading [intoning] the First Stasis of the Oikoi [of the Akathist Hymn, Oikoi 1–6: A–Z / Ἀ–Ζ], censing thrice at the end of each Ikos. Upon completing the First Stasis of the Oikoi, the Patriarch kisses the Holy Icon, and, turning toward the people, blesses them with the Trikirion [three-candle candelabra], while the Choirs sing **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*). The Patriarch then enters the Holy Sanctuary. After removing his Epitrachilion and Omophorion, preceded by the Primikirios and the two Deacons, he exits the Holy Sanctuary and ascends his Throne. The Primikirios then, after making a sign, withdraws back into the Holy Sanctuary. The Great Archdeacon and the Second Deacon, having laid aside their vestments and the Dikerotrikera, come out from the Holy Sanctuary and stand by the Patriarchal Throne. The Choirs, in the meantime, sing the First and Third Odes of the Canon: **"I shall open my mouth and it shall be filled with the Spirit..."** (*Ἀνοίξω τὸ στόμα μου*). Upon their completion, the two Choirs together sing the Kontakion in Tone 8: **"To the Champion General"** (*Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ*) slowly and melodiously. During the penultimate verse of this hymn, the Great Ecclesiarch approaches the first of the officiating Hierarchs and invites him through a sign to enter the Holy

---

*** *Note on the Sign:* A clergyman makes a sign (*σχῆμα*) to another clergyman during a service by placing his right hand on his chest and bowing slightly.  
**** *Note on Dikerotrikera:* The candelabra with the three candles symbolizes the three Persons of the Holy Trinity. The candelabra with the two candles symbolizes the dual nature of Christ as God and Man...[^488]

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (Stases 2–4 and Canon Odes 4–9)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τοῦ Ἀκαθίστου Ὕμνου (Συνέχεια)

πληρουμένου δὲ τοῦ μέλους, ἐξέρχεται τοῦ ἱεροῦ Βήματος, προπορευομένου τοῦ Τριτεύοντος καὶ ἑνὸς Διακόνου μετὰ δικηροτρικηρίων καὶ ἵσταται ἐνώπιον τῆς ἁγίας Εἰκόνος καὶ θυμιάσας τὸν Πατριάρχην καὶ τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα ἀνὰ τρίς, ἄρχεται ἀναγινώσκειν τὴν β΄ τῶν Οἴκων Στάσιν κατὰ τὴν ἀνωτέρω τάξιν. Πληρωθέντων τῶν Οἴκων, ἀσπάζεται τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα καὶ ποιήσας σχῆμα τῷ Πατριάρχῃ, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἅγιον Βῆμα καὶ ἀποτιθέμενος τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ τὸ Ὠμοφόριον, παραμένει ἐν τῷ Ἱερῷ· οἱ δὲ χοροὶ ψάλλουσι τοῦ Κανόνος τὴν δ΄, ε΄ καὶ ς΄ Ὠδὴν καθ’ ἣν ἐξέρχεται αὖθις ὁ Ἐκκλησιάρχης καὶ προσκαλεῖ διὰ σχήματος τὸν δεύτερον τῶν χοροστατούντων Ἀρχιερέα, ὅστις λαβὼν ἐπίσης καιρὸν εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερὸν προπορευομένου τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου, ἐνδύεται τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ τὸ Ὠμοφόριον καὶ περὶ τὸ τέλος τοῦ συντόμου Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ ἐξέρχεται μετὰ τῶν αὐτῶν Διακόνων καὶ θυμιάσας τὸν Πατριάρχην τρὶς καὶ τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα ἀναγινώσκει τὴν γ΄ τῶν Οἴκων Στάσιν, ἧς πληρωθείσης ποιεῖ καὶ αὐτὸς ὅ, τι ὁ προηγηθεὶς καὶ μένει ἐν τῷ Ἱερῷ· οἱ δὲ Χοροὶ ψάλλουσι τοῦ Κανόνος τὴν ζ΄, η΄ καὶ θ΄ Ὠδήν. Ψαλλομένης τῆς ζ΄ Ὠδῆς, ἐξελθόντες τοῦ Ἱεροῦ οἱ δύο ἐνδεδυμένοι Διάκονοι θυμιῶσι κατὰ τὴν τάξιν τὸν λαόν, κατὰ δὲ τὴν θ΄ Ὠδὴν ἐξέρχεται αὖθις ὁ Ἐκκλησιάρχης καὶ προσκαλεῖ διὰ σχήματος τὸν τρίτον τῶν Ἀρχιερέων, ὃς καὶ λαβὼν ὡσαύτως καιρόν, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἱερὸν Βῆμα μετὰ τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου, ἐνδύεται τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ Ὠμοφόριον καὶ περὶ τὰ τέλη τοῦ Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ, ἐξέρχεται μετὰ τῶν αὐτῶν Διακόνων, καὶ θυμιάσας τὸν Πατριάρχην τρίς, καὶ τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα, ἀναγινώσκει τὴν δ΄ τῶν Οἴκων Στάσιν, ἧς πληρωθείσης ποιεῖ καὶ αὐτὸς ὡς οἱ ἄλλοι δύο Ἀρχιερεῖς· τότε οἱ μένοντες ἐν τῷ χορῷ Ἀρχιερεῖς κατὰ τάξιν ὁ εἷς κατόπιν τοῦ ἄλλου προσερχόμενοι τῷ Πατριάρχῃ ποιοῦσι σχῆμα καὶ ἀσπαζόμενοι τὴν ἁγίαν Εἰκόνα εἰσέρχονται εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν, μετὰ δὲ τὸ τελευταῖον σύντομον Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ ὁ Ἀναγνώστης λέγει τὸ Τρισάγιον καὶ τὸ Κοντάκιον, ὁ δὲ Πριμικήριος μετὰ τοῦ Διβαμβούλου ἐλθὼν ἵσταται κατὰ τὴν τάξιν ἀπέναντι τοῦ

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (Stases 2–4 and Canon Odes 4–9)

## Service of the Akathist Hymn Continued (Stases 2–4 and Canon Odes 4–9)

Sanctuary. This Hierarch, after kissing the Patriarch's hand, enters into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], preceded by the Ecclesiarch, and vests himself with the Epitrachilion and the Omophorion. When the above hymn is completed, this Hierarch exits the Holy Sanctuary, preceded by the Third Deacon and one other Deacon with Dikerotrikera, and stands before the Holy Icon. Censing the Patriarch and the Holy Icon three times each, he begins to read [intone] the Second Stasis of the Oikoi [Oikoi 7–12: H–M / Η–Μ], according to the order described above [as with the First Stasis]. Having completed the Second Stasis of the Oikoi, the Hierarch kisses the Holy Icon, and, making a sign to the Patriarch, returns to the Holy Sanctuary. Having taken off the Epitrachilion and the Omophorion, he remains there. The Choirs then sing the Fourth, Fifth, and Sixth Odes of the Canon: **"I shall open my mouth and it shall be filled with the Spirit..."** (*Ἀνοίξω τὸ στόμα μου*). During the Sixth Ode, the Ecclesiarch again exits the Holy Sanctuary and calls forth with a sign the second of the officiating Hierarchs. The second Hierarch also, after receiving Keron from the Patriarch, enters the Holy Sanctuary, preceded by the Ecclesiarch, and vests himself with the Epitrachilion and the Omophorion. Toward the end of the more quickly sung Kontakion: **"To the Champion General"** (*Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ*), he exits the Holy Sanctuary with the same Deacons. Having censed the Patriarch three times, as well as the Holy Icon, he reads [intones] the Third Stasis of the Oikoi [Oikoi 13–18: N–P / Ν–Π]. When he has completed the Third Stasis, he too does as the preceding [first] Hierarch: he returns to the Holy Sanctuary, takes off the Epitrachilion and the Omophorion, and remains there. The Choirs then chant the Seventh, Eighth, and Ninth Odes of the Canon. While the Seventh Ode is being sung, the two vested Deacons exit the Holy Sanctuary and cense the people according to the prescribed order. During the singing of the Ninth Ode, the Ecclesiarch exits the Holy Sanctuary again and calls forth with a sign the third of the officiating Hierarchs. This Hierarch, as the previous two, similarly receives Keron from the Patriarch and vests with the Epitrachilion and the Omophorion. Toward the end of the hymn: **"To the Champion General"** (*Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ*), he exits the Holy Sanctuary with the same Deacons. He censes the Patriarch three times, as well as the Holy Icon, and reads [intones] the Fourth Stasis of the Oikoi [Oikoi 19–24: T–Omega / Τ–Ω, repeating Ikos 1: A / Ἀ]. Upon completing the Fourth Stasis, this Hierarch also does as the previous two Hierarchs. Then the rest of the Hierarchs in the group, coming in order one after the other before the Patriarch, make a sign to him and, after kissing the Holy Icon, enter the Holy Sanctuary. After the last and rapidly sung **"To the Champion General"** (*Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ*), the Reader recites the Trisagion and the Kontakion: **"To the Champion General"**. Then, the Primikirios with the Divamvulon [a large candlestick with two bulbous...[^489]

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Concluded (§ Appendix 4)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τοῦ Ἀκαθίστου Ὕμνου (Τέλος)

Πατριάρχου, ἐπίσης καὶ οἱ Διάκονοι πάντες ἐξελθόντες τῆς νοτίου τοῦ Ἱεροῦ Πύλης μετὰ τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου καὶ τῶν Ἱερέων, λαμβάνουσι τὰς οἰκείας θέσεις, ἤτοι ὁ μὲν Ἐκκλησιάρχης καὶ οἱ Ἱερεῖς ἵστανται ἀπέναντι τοῦ Πατριάρχου, οἱ δὲ Διάκονοι ἐκ δεξιῶν τοῦ Θρόνου· ὁ Ἀναγνώστης Ἱερεὺς ἐξακολουθεῖ λέγων μετὰ τὸ Κοντάκιον Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ τὸ Κύριε ἐλέησον μ΄ καὶ τὸ Ὁ ἐν παντὶ καιρῷ, ὁ Πατριάρχης τὸ Ὁ Θεὸς οἰκτιρήσαι ἡμᾶς καὶ οἱ Κανονάρχαι τὸ Ἄσπιλε ἀμόλυντε καὶ τὸ Καὶ δὸς ἡμῖν, Δέσποτα. Μετὰ ταῦτα ὁ Ἱερεὺς λέγει τὴν μικρὰν Ἀπόλυσιν καὶ Εὐξώμεθα. Εἰς τὸ τέλος δὲ οἱ Χοροὶ ψάλλουσι μετὰ μέλους τὸ Κάθισμα Τὴν ὡραιότητα τῆς παρθενίας σου· τούτου πληρωθέντος, ὁ Πατριάρχης κατέρχεται τοῦ Θρόνου καὶ εὐλογήσας ἐξέρχεται, τοῦ Ἱερέως ἐκφωνοῦντος ἐκ τοῦ ἱεροῦ Βήματος τὸ Δι' εὐχῶν τοῦ ἁγίου Δεσπότου ἡμῶν, Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς. Ἀμήν.*****

----------
***** Πλὴν αὐτῆς τῆς κατακλείσεως λέγεται [ἔστιν ὅτε καὶ ὁ] Πατριάρχης ἐκφωνεῖ· Δι' εὐχῶν τῶν Ἁγίων Πατέρων ἡμῶν, Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς. Ἀμήν.

# Appendix: Service of the Akathist Hymn Concluded (§ Appendix 4)

## Service of the Akathist Hymn Concluded (§ Appendix 4)

...shaped knobs and a candle in between them] comes and stands, according to the order of service, opposite the Patriarch. Also, all the Deacons, exiting from the South Gate of the Sanctuary [Bema] with the Ecclesiarch and the Priests, take their designated positions: the Ecclesiarch and the Priests opposite the Patriarch, and the Deacons on the right side of the Patriarchal Throne. The Reader-Priest continues reciting after the Kontakion in Tone 8: **"To the Champion General"** (*Τῇ ὑπερμάχῳ*), the **"Lord, have mercy"** (*Κύριε, ἐλέησον*) 40 times, and the prayer **"Thou Who at all times and at every hour..."** (*Ὁ ἐν παντὶ καιρῷ*). Then the Patriarch recites: **"May God be merciful unto us and bless us..."** (*Ὁ Θεὸς οἰκτιρήσαι ἡμᾶς*). The Canonarchs then intone the prayers: **"O Spotless, Undefiled, Incorrupt..."** (*Ἄσπιλε, ἀμόλυντε*) and **"And grant unto us, O Master, as we depart unto sleep..."** (*Καὶ δὸς ἡμῖν, Δέσποτα*). After these prayers, the Priest pronounces the Small Dismissal and the petition **"Let us pray for the peace of the world..."** (*Εὐξώμεθα ὑπὲρ εἰρήνης τοῦ κόσμου*). At the end, the Choirs sing melodiously the Kathisma in Tone 3: **"Seeing the beauty of thy virginity..."** (*Τὴν ὡραιότητα τῆς παρθενίας σου*). Upon the completion of this hymn, the Patriarch descends from the Throne, and, blessing the people, departs from the Church, while the Priest intones from the Holy Sanctuary [Bema]: **"Through the prayers of our holy Master, Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us. Amen."** (*Δι' εὐχῶν τοῦ ἁγίου Δεσπότου ἡμῶν, Κύριε Ἰησοῦ Χριστὲ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς. Ἀμήν.*)*****

----------
***** [Note on the Dismissal: At some point before this exclamation by the Priest, or when the Patriarch officiates, the Patriarch exclaims: "Through the prayers of our Holy Fathers, Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us. Amen."]*[^490]

# Appendix: The Order of the Service of the Holy Passion in the Patriarchal Church

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν

Η  
ΕΝ ΤΩ ΠΑΤΡΙΑΡΧΙΚΩ ΝΑΩ  
ΤΑΞΙΣ ΤΗΣ ΑΚΟΛΟΥΘΙΑΣ  
ΤΩΝ ΑΓΙΩΝ ΠΑΘΩΝ  
----------  

Τῇ ἁγίᾳ καὶ Μεγάλῃ Πέμπτῃ ἑσπέρας, περὶ τὴν ιβ΄ ὥραν κρούουσιν οἱ κώδωνες, ἡ δὲ Αὐτοῦ Θ. Παναγιότης [ὁ Πατριάρχης] κατέρχεται [ἐν τῷ Ναῷ] περὶ τὴν ἡμίσειαν ὥραν μετὰ τῆς ἀκολούθου παρατάξεως. Προπορευομένων τεσσάρων κλητήρων ῥαβδούχων καὶ ἀγγελλόντων τὴν ἔλευσιν τοῦ Πατριάρχου, ἕπεται ἅπας ὁ ἱερὸς τῶν ἐφημερίων κλῆρος μετὰ τοῦ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτου βαδίζων στοιχηδὸν πρὸ τοῦ Πατριάρχου ὡς καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχης φέρων τὴν πατριαρχικὴν ποιμαντικὴν ῥάβδον· τούτοις δὲ ἕπεται ὁ Πατριάρχης κρατῶν χαζράνιον, συμβαδίζουσι δὲ τῷ Πατριάρχῃ ὁ Μ. Πρωτοσύγκελλος ἐκ δεξιῶν καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος ἐξ εὐωνύμων, καὶ ἕπεται ὁ Δευτερεύων, ὁ Τριτεύων καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ τῶν Διακόνων· ὅταν φθάσωσιν ἔμπροσθεν τῆς Μ. πύλης τοῦ Ναοῦ, ὁ Πατριάρχης ἐνδύεται παρὰ τοῦ Μ. Ἀρχιδιακόνου τὸν Μανδύαν, ὁ δὲ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχης δίδωσι τῷ Πρωτοσυγκέλλῳ τὴν ῥάβδον καὶ οὗτος ἐγχειρίζει ταύτην τῷ Πατριάρχῃ ἀσπαζόμενος τὴν δεξιὰν αὐτοῦ, διὰ τῆς αὐτῆς δὲ τάξεως εἰσέρχονται ἅπαντες εἰς τὸν Ναὸν προπορευομένου τοῦ Μ. Πριμικηρίου φέροντος τὸ μέγα κηροπήγιον (διβάμβουλον). Φθάσας ὁ Πατριάρχης ἐν τῷ μέσῳ τοῦ Ναοῦ εὐλογεῖ τὸν λαόν, τῶν Χορῶν

# Appendix: The Order of the Service of the Holy Passion in the Patriarchal Church

## The Order of the Service of the Holy Passion in the Patriarchal Church

# THE ORDER OF THE SERVICE OF THE HOLY PASSION

## AS SERVED IN THE PATRIARCHAL CHURCH

---

On Holy and Great Thursday evening, about the Twelfth Hour [6:00 p.m.], the bells ring. About a half hour later His All-Holiness the Patriarch comes down into the Church with his customary entourage. Preceding him are four staff-bearing ushers announcing the arrival of the Patriarch. Then all the sacred clergy of the parish priests of the Church, with the Great Archimandrite, walk row by row before the Patriarch, as does also the Great Ecclesiarch carrying the Patriarchal Pastoral Staff (*Pateritsa*). After these comes the Patriarch holding a Chazranion [an unadorned wooden walking staff].* Walking with the Patriarch are the Great Protosynkellos [Grand Chancellor] on his right side and the Great Archdeacon on his left; then follow the Second Deacon, the Third Deacon, and the rest of the Deacons. When they arrive before the Great Gate of the Church, the Patriarch is vested by the Great Archdeacon with the Mandyas [Hierarchical Mantle]. The Great Ecclesiarch gives the Pastoral Staff to the Protosynkellos, who in turn hands it over to the Patriarch, kissing his right hand. In this same order, all of them enter into the Church, preceded by the Great Primikirios [Leader of Processions and participant in the chanting of the chanters], who carries the great candlestick with two bulbous knobs [the Divamvulon]. Arriving in the middle of the Nave, the Patriarch blesses the people, while the Choirs chant **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*)...

---

* *Note on the Chazranion:* A plain walking staff appropriate in its lack of ornamentation to the solemnity of Great and Holy Week.[^491]

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Opening Prayers, Hexapsalmos, and Troparion)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν (Συνέχεια)

ψαλλόντων τὸ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη καὶ ἀνέρχεται εἰς τὸν Θρόνον· ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης τότε, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος καὶ ὁ Δευτερεύων λαμβάνουσι καιρὸν παρὰ τοῦ Πατριάρχου ἕπονται δὲ ὁ Τριτεύων καὶ οἱ λοιποὶ Διάκονοι καὶ εἰσέρχονται εἰς τὸ ἅγιον Βῆμα οἱ μὲν διὰ τῆς τοῦ βορείου κλίτους πύλης, οἱ δὲ διὰ τῆς τοῦ νοτίου, καὶ μένει παρὰ τῷ Πατριάρχῃ μόνος ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος· ὁ δὲ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης, στὰς ἐνώπιον τῆς ἁγίας Τραπέζης μετ’ Ἐπιτραχηλίου ἐκφωνεῖ Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, καὶ ὁ Πατριάρχης ἄρχεται Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν δόξα σοι· Βασιλεῦ Οὐράνιε Παράκλητε κτλ., ὁ δὲ ταχθεὶς Ἀναγνώστης Ἱερεὺς ἱστάμενος ἀπέναντι τοῦ Πατριάρχου ἄρχεται ἀναγινώσκειν τὸ Τρισάγιον μετὰ μέλους καὶ τοὺς πρὸ τοῦ Ἑξαψάλμου δύο ψαλμούς· ἀρχομένου δὲ τοῦ α΄ ψαλμοῦ, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης ἐξέρχεται τῆς βορείου τοῦ Ἱεροῦ πύλης, προπορευομένου λαμπαδούχου, ἐνδεδυμένος Φελώνιον μετ’ Ἐπιτραχηλίου καὶ φέρων θυμιατὸν (Κατζὶ), θυμιᾷ, ὡς εἴθισται, ἅπαντα τὸν λαὸν καὶ ἐπιστρέψας ἵσταται ἐνώπιον τῆς Ὡραίας Πύλης καὶ ἐκφωνεῖ μετὰ τὸ δεύτερον Τρισάγιον, τὸ Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία, ὁ Ἀναγνώστης εἶτα τὸ Σῶσον Κύριε τὸν λαόν σου καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ δύο Τροπάρια· ὁ δὲ Ἀρχιμανδρίτης Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεὸς κτλ. καὶ ἀκολούθως τὴν ἐκφώνησιν Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος, καὶ εὐθὺς Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ καὶ ὁμοουσίῳ καὶ ζωοποιῷ καὶ ἀδιαιρέτῳ Τριάδι πάντοτε νῦν καὶ ἀεὶ καὶ εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων καὶ ὁ Πατριάρχης ἄρχεται τοῦ Ἑξαψάλμου· τούτου πληρωθέντος, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης λέγει τὴν Μ, Συναπτὴν καὶ μετὰ τὴν Συναπτὴν ἄρχονται οἱ Χοροὶ ψάλλειν τὸ Ἀλληλούϊα μετὰ μέλους τετράκις, λέγοντες καὶ τοὺς συνήθεις Στίχους Ἐκ νυκτὸς ὀρθρίζει· Δικαιοσύνην μάθετε· Πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακά· Ζῆλος λήψεται λαόν, καὶ τὸ Ὅτε οἱ ἔνδοξοι μαθηταί, δὶς μὲν ἀργῶς ἅπαξ δὲ ἀργοσυντόμως· [ὁ Βιολάκης λανθασμένως ἔχει ἐδῶ ἀντιστρέψει τὴν σειρὰν τῶν δύο τελευταίων στίχων] τὰ δύο τελευταῖα λέγονται σὺν τῷ Δόξα, καὶ νῦν ἀρχυμένου τοῦ μέλους, οἱ μέλλοντες χοροστατῆσαι Ἀρχιερεῖς ἐξέρχονται τῆς νοτίου τοῦ Ἱεροῦ Βήματος πύλης προπορευομένου τοῦ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχου καὶ ἵστανται ἕκαστος κατὰ τάξιν ἐν τῇ οἰκείᾳ αὐτοῦ θέσει, ὁ δὲ Ἐκκλησιάρχης ποιήσας σχῆμα τῷ Πατριάρχῃ εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἱερὸν Βῆμα, εὐθὺς δὲ καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος ποιήσας σχῆμα εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν, ἵνα ἐνδυθῇ ἀφεὶς εἰς τὴν θέσιν του ἕνα τῶν Διακόνων· ἀρχομένου τοῦ β΄ Χοροῦ ψάλλειν τὸ β΄ Ὅτε οἱ ἔνδοξοι Μαθηταί, ὁ μὲν Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τῶν λοιπῶν διακόνων ἐξέρχονται ἀπὸ

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Opening Prayers, Hexapsalmos, and Troparion)

## Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Opening Prayers, Hexapsalmos, and Troparion)

...**"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*), and he ascends the Patriarchal Throne. Then the Great Archimandrite, the Great Archdeacon, and the Second Deacon receive Keron [the liturgical permission and blessing to officiate] from the Patriarch; then follow the Third Deacon and the rest of the Deacons. They enter into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], the former group by the North Deacon's Gate and the latter group by the South Deacon's Gate; only the Great Archdeacon remains beside the Patriarch. The Great Archimandrite, standing before the Holy Table and wearing the Epitrachilion, exclaims: **"Blessed is our God always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*); and the Patriarch begins: **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee!"**, **"O Heavenly King, Comforter, Spirit of truth..."** (*Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι... Βασιλεῦ οὐράνιε, Παράκλητε...*). The designated Reader-Priest, standing opposite the Patriarch, begins to recite melodiously the Trisagion Prayers and the two Psalms before the Six Psalms [Psalms 19 and 20 LXX]. As the first Psalm begins, the Great Archimandrite, vested in Phelonion with Epitrachilion and preceded by a candle-bearer, exits through the North Gate of the Sanctuary carrying a hand censer (*Katzion*). He censes, as is customary, all the people, and, returning, stands before the Royal Doors [Beautiful Gate] and exclaims after the second Trisagion: **"For Thine is the kingdom, and the power, and the glory, of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία*). The Reader then recites the Troparion **"O Lord, save Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance..."** (*Σῶσον, Κύριε, τὸν λαόν σου*) and the other two Troparia after it. The Archimandrite intones: **"Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy..."** (*Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς, ὁ Θεός*), and following that, the Exclamation: **"For Thou art a merciful God and lovest mankind, and unto Thee do we send up glory, to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος*). And immediately: **"Glory to the holy, consubstantial, life-creating, and undivided Trinity, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Δόξα τῇ ἁγίᾳ καὶ ὁμοουσίῳ...*). The Patriarch then begins the Six Psalms (*Hexapsalmos*). When these are completed, the Great Archimandrite says the Great Litany, and after the Litany the Choirs begin to sing the **"Alleluia"** (*Ἀλληλούϊα*) melodiously four times, chanting also the customary verses beforehand: 1. **"My spirit seeketh Thee early in the morning watches, O God, for Thy commandments are a light upon the earth."** (*Ἐκ νυκτὸς ὀρθρίζει...*, Isa. 26:9 LXX); 2. **"Learn righteousness, O ye inhabitants of the earth."** (*Δικαιοσύνην μάθετε...*, Isa. 26:9 LXX); 3. **"Zeal shall seize an uninstructed people."** (*Ζῆλος λήψεται λαόν*, Isa. 26:11 LXX); 4. **"Bring more evils upon them, O Lord, bring more evils upon them."** (*Πρόσθες αὐτοῖς κακά, Κύριε*, Isa. 26:15 LXX). [Here, in an apparent editorial slip, Violakis in the Greek text reversed the sequence of the last two verses]. Then we chant the Troparion in Tone 8: **"When the glorious disciples were enlightened at the washing of the feet..."** (*Ὅτε οἱ ἔνδοξοι μαθηταί*): twice slowly, and once moderately fast (*ἀργοσυντόμως*). The last two repetitions are sung with **"Glory..."** and **"Now and forever..."** At the beginning of the chanting of this hymn, the Hierarchs who are to participate in the service exit the Holy Sanctuary through the South Gate, preceded by the Great Ecclesiarch, and stand each in his designated position according to rank. The Great Ecclesiarch, having made a reverent sign (*σχῆμα*) to the Patriarch, enters into the Holy Sanctuary. Immediately also the Great Archdeacon, having made a reverent sign to the Patriarch, enters into the Holy Sanctuary in order to vest, leaving one of the other Deacons in his place. As the Second Choir begins to chant for the second time the Troparion: **"When the glorious disciples..."**, the Archdeacon with the rest of the...[^492]

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 1 and 2, Antiphons)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν (Συνέχεια)

τῆς Ὡραίας Πύλης ἐνδεδυμένοι τὰς ἱερὰς αὐτῶν στολάς, καὶ κρατοῦντες ὁ μὲν Ἀρχιδιάκονος καὶ ὁ Δευτερεύων τὰ τρικήρια, ὁ δὲ Τριτεύων καὶ ὁ δ΄ τῶν Διακόνων τὰ δικήρια, ἵστανται περὶ τὸν θρόνον, ἐκ δεξιῶν μὲν ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τοῦ Τριτεύοντος, ἐξ ἀριστερῶν δὲ ὁ Δευτερεύων καὶ ὁ ἕτερος· ἀρχομένου δὲ τοῦ α΄ Χοροῦ ψάλλειν τὸ τρίτον Ὅτε οἱ ἔνδοξοι Μαθηταὶ ἀργοσυντόμως, ὁ Πατριάρχης κατέρχεται τοῦ θρόνου, καὶ προπορευομένων τοῦ Ἐκκλησιάρχου, τοῦ Πριμικηρίου καὶ τῶν Διακόνων εἰσέρχεται διὰ τῆς Ὡραίας Πύλης εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν, καὶ ἐνδύεται Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ Ὠμοφόριον. Εἶτα τιθέασιν ἔμπροσθεν τῆς Ὡραίας πύλης τὸ Τρισκέλιον καὶ ἐπ’ αὐτοῦ τὸ ἱερὸν Εὐαγγέλιον, καὶ οἱ μὲν Διάκονοι ἐξερχόμενοι ἵστανται ἀνὰ δύο περὶ αὐτὸ κρατοῦντες τὰ δικηροτρίκηρα, ὁ δὲ Πριμικήριος μετὰ τῆς Λαμπάδος ἀπέναντι τῆς Ὡραίας πύλης· πληρωθέντος δὲ τοῦ μέλους, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος ἐκφωνεῖ Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς τῆς ἀκροάσεως τοῦ ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου κτλ., ὁ Πατριάρχης Εἰρήνη πᾶσι, οἱ χοροὶ Καὶ τῷ πνεύματί σου, ὁ Πατριάρχης Ἐκ τοῦ κατὰ Ἰωάννην ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου τὸ ἀνάγνωσμα, ὁ Ἀρχιδιάκονος Πρόσχωμεν· καὶ ἀναγινώσκει τὸ [Α΄] ἱερὸν Εὐαγγέλιον τῆς Καινῆς Διαθήκης, τοῦ λαοῦ ἐν πάσῃ εὐλαβείᾳ καὶ προσοχῇ ἀκρωμένου· τούτου πληρωθέντος, εὐλογήσας τὸν λαὸν διὰ τρικηρίου, τῶν Χορῶν ψαλλόντων τὸ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ ἱερὸν Βῆμα καὶ ἀποθέσας τὸ Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ τὸ Ὠμοφόριον ἐπὶ τῆς ἁγίας Τραπέζης ἐξέρχεται, προπορευομένου τοῦ Πριμικηρίου καὶ τῶν δύο Διακόνων, καὶ ἵσταται αὖθις ἐπὶ τοῦ θρόνου, ὁ δὲ Πριμικήριος ποιήσας σχῆμα τῷ Πατριάρχῃ, ἀποχωρεῖ, ταυτοχρόνως δὲ καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μετὰ τοῦ Δευτερεύοντος ἀποχωροῦσι καὶ ἐκδυθέντες τὰς ἱερετικὰς αὐτῶν στολὰς ἐξέρχονται καὶ ἵστανται παρὰ τὸν θρόνον τοῦ Πατριάρχου. Ἀρχομένων δὲ τῶν Χορῶν ψάλλειν τὰ Ἀντίφωνα, εἰς τὸ β΄ Ἀντίφωνον ὁ Μ. Ἐκκλησιάρχης μετὰ τοῦ Τριτεύοντος καὶ τοῦ ἑτέρου τῶν Διακόνων ἐξέρχονται τῆς Ὡραίας Πύλης, καὶ οἱ μὲν ἵστανται περὶ αὐτήν, ὁ δὲ Ἐκκλησιάρχης προσέρχεται τῷ πρωτοθρόνῳ Ἀρχιερεῖ καὶ προσκαλεῖ αὐτὸν διὰ σχήματος· οὗτος δὲ ἀσπασάμενος τὴν χεῖρα τοῦ Πατριάρχου εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερὸν προπορευομένου τοῦ τε Ἐκκλησιάρχου καὶ τῶν Διακόνων, οἵτινες ἐξέρχονται αὖθις καὶ ἵστανται περὶ τὸ Τρισκέλιον κρατοῦντες δικηροτρίκηρα, ὁ δὲ ἐνδύεται Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ Ὠμοφόριον· πληρωθέντος δὲ τοῦ Καθίσματος Ἐν τῷ δείπνῳ τοὺς μαθητὰς διατρέφων, ὁ Τριτεύων ἐκφωνεῖ Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς, ὁ Πατριάρχης Εἰρήνη πᾶσι, οἱ Χοροὶ Καὶ μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματός σου, ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ἔρχεται παρὰ τῷ Τρισκελίῳ καὶ ἀναγινώσκει τὸ Β΄ Εὐαγγέλιον, οὗ πληρωθέντος, ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ψάλλει συντόμως τὸ Δόξα τῇ μακροθυμίᾳ σου, Κύριε, δόξα σοι, ὅπερ ψάλλεται

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 1 and 2, Antiphons)

## Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 1 and 2, Antiphons)

...Deacons, dressed in their sacred vestments and carrying the Dikirotrikera, exit from the Holy Sanctuary through the Royal Doors [Beautiful Gate]. The Great Archdeacon and the Second Deacon hold Trikiria [three-candle candelabra]; the Third and the Fourth Deacons hold Dikiria [two-candle candelabra]. To the right of the Patriarchal Throne stand the Great Archdeacon with the Third Deacon; to the left, the Second Deacon and the fourth Deacon. As the First Choir begins to chant for the third time the Troparion: **"When the glorious disciples were enlightened..."** (*Ὅτε οἱ ἔνδοξοι μαθηταί*) at a moderate tempo, the Patriarch descends from the Throne, and, preceded by the Ecclesiarch, the Primikirios, and the Deacons, enters through the Royal Doors into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], and vests with Epitrachilion and Omophorion. Then they place before the Royal Doors the Triskelion [three-legged analogion], and upon it the Holy Gospel Book. The Deacons, coming out from the Sanctuary, stand two by two around the stand holding the Dikirotrikera, and the Primikirios with the candle stands opposite the Royal Doors. When the hymn is completed, the Great Archdeacon exclaims: **"And that we may be accounted worthy to hear the Holy Gospel, let us pray to the Lord our God."** (*Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς...*). The Patriarch intones: **"Peace be unto all."** (*Εἰρήνη πᾶσι*). The Choirs respond: **"And with thy spirit."** (*Καὶ τῷ πνεύματί σου*). The Patriarch: **"The reading is from the Holy Gospel according to John."** (*Ἐκ τοῦ κατὰ Ἰωάννην ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου τὸ ἀνάγνωσμα*). The Archdeacon: **"Let us attend!"** (*Πρόσχωμεν*). And the Patriarch reads the First Holy Gospel of the New Testament [John 13:31–18:1], the congregation listening with all reverence and attention. When this is completed, having blessed the people with the Trikirion while the Choirs sing **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*), he enters into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], and, having laid aside the Epitrachilion and Omophorion upon the Holy Table, exits preceded by the Primikirios and the two Deacons, and stands again upon the Throne. The Primikirios, having made a reverent sign (*σχῆμα*) to the Patriarch, withdraws; at the same time, the Great Archdeacon and the Second Deacon also withdraw, and, having unvested from their sacred vestments, come out and stand beside the Patriarchal Throne. As the Choirs begin chanting the Antiphons, at the Second Antiphon the Great Ecclesiarch with the Third Deacon and the other Deacon exit through the Royal Doors: the Deacons stand on either side of the Doors, while the Ecclesiarch approaches the ranking senior Hierarch and invites him by making a reverent sign. This Hierarch, having kissed the hand of the Patriarch, enters into the Holy Sanctuary preceded by the Ecclesiarch and the Deacons. The Deacons exit once again and stand around the Triskelion holding Dikirotrikera, while the Hierarch vests with Epitrachilion and Omophorion. At the completion of the Kathisma in Tone 7: **"While feeding the disciples at the Supper..."** (*Ἐν τῷ δείπνῳ τοὺς μαθητὰς διατρέφων*), the Third Deacon exclaims: **"And that we may be accounted worthy to hear the Holy Gospel..."** (*Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς*). The Patriarch: **"Peace be unto all."** (*Εἰρήνη πᾶσι*). The Choirs: **"And with thy spirit."** (*Καὶ μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματός σου*). The Hierarch comes before the Triskelion and reads the Second Gospel [John 18:1–28]. When this is completed, the First Choir sings rapidly: **"Glory to Thy forbearance, O Lord, glory to Thee!"** (*Δόξα τῇ μακροθυμίᾳ σου, Κύριε, δόξα σοι*), which is sung...[^493]

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 3–6, Procession of the Crucified)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν (Συνέχεια)

ἐναλλὰξ ὑπὸ τῶν Χορῶν εἰς ἕκαστον τῶν Εὐαγγελίων, ἐξαιρουμένου τοῦ τελευταίου· ἡ δὲ πρὸς τοὺς χοροστατοῦντας καὶ μέλλοντας ἀναπρόσκλησις ἐπαναλαμβάνεται εἰς ἕνα ἕκαστον τῶν Ἀρχιερέων κατὰ τάξιν μέχρι τοῦ τελευταίου. Μετὰ τοὺς Ἀρχιερεῖς τὰ ἐναπολειπόμενα Εὐαγγέλια ἀναγινώσκονται ὑπὸ τοῦ Μ. Πρωτοσυγκέλλου, τοῦ Ἀρχιγραμματέως, τοῦ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτου, τοῦ Συγκέλλου καὶ καθεξῆς· διὰ τούτους ὅμως οἱ Διάκονοι ἔσωθεν τοῦ Βήματος ἱστάμενοι ἐκφωνοῦσιν ἐκ περιτροπῆς τὸ Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς. Πληρωθέντος τοῦ Β΄ Εὐαγγελίου, ἄρχεται ὁ β΄ χορὸς ψάλλειν τὸ δ΄ Ἀντίφωνον μέχρι τοῦ Καθίσματος Ποῖός σε τρόπος Ἰούδα, καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Γ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον· μετὰ τὸ τέλος, ὁ β΄ Χορὸς τὸ Δόξα τῇ μακροθυμίᾳ σου Κύριε, καὶ ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ψάλλει τὸ ζ΄ Ἀντίφωνον μέχρι τοῦ Καθίσματος Ὢ πῶς Ἰούδας ὁ ποτὲ μαθητὴς καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Δ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον. Εἶτα ὁ β΄ Χορὸς τὸ ι΄ Ἀντίφωνον μέχρι τοῦ Καθίσματος Ὅτε παρέστης τῷ Καϊάφᾳ ὁ Θεὸς καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ [Ε΄] Εὐαγγέλιον· μετὰ τοῦτο , ὁ α΄ Χορὸς τὸ ιγ΄ Ἀντίφωνον μέχρι τοῦ θεοτοκίου τροπαρίου Χαῖρε ἡ δι’ Ἀγγέλου τὴν χαρὰν τοῦ κόσμου δεξαμένη· τότε ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης, ἐνδεδυμένος Ἐπιτραχήλιον καὶ Φελώνιον, αἴρει τὸν Ἐσταυρωμένον καὶ ποιήσας κύκλον περὶ τὴν ἁγίαν Τράπεζαν, ψάλλων κατὰ τὸ ὕφος τοῦ Εὐαγγελίου ἐν κατανύξει τὸ Ἰδιόμελον Σήμερον κρεμᾶται ἐπὶ ξύλου, ἐξέρχεται τῆς βορείου τοῦ ἱεροῦ Βήματος πύλης προπορευομένων τοῦ Πριμικηρίου, τῶν Λαμπαδούχων, καὶ δύο Διακόνων θυμιώντων καὶ φθάνει εἰς τὸ μέσον τοῦ Ναοῦ, ἔνθα πήγνυται ὁ Σταυρός1· ὁ δὲ α΄ Χορὸς ἐπαναλαμβάνει τὸ αὐτὸ Ἰδιόμελον ψάλλων αὐτὸ εἰς τὸν ἦχον του· εἶτα ὁ β΄ Χορὸς Μὴ ὡς Ἰουδαῖοι ἑορτάσωμεν κτλ. μέχρι τοῦ Καθίσματος Ἐξηγόρασας ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς κατάρας τοῦ Νόμου καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ ΣΤ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον. Μετὰ ταῦτα ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ἄρχεται τῶν Μακαρισμῶν Ἐν τῇ βασιλείᾳ σου μνήσθητι ἡμῶν, Κύριε, ὁ δὲ β΄ Χορὸς ἐρχόμενος εἰς τὸν Μακαρισμὸν Μακάριοι οἱ πεινῶντες καὶ διψῶντες τὴν δικαιοσύνην ψάλλει τὸ

----------
1. Ἐν τῷ πατριαρχικῷ ναῷ (ὡς καὶ ἐν Ἁγίῳ Ὄρει μέχρι τοῦδε) ἡ ἔξοδος αὕτη τοῦ Ἐσταυρωμένου οὐκ ἐγίνετο μέχρι τῆς πατριαρχείας Σωφρονίου τοῦ ἀπὸ Ἀμασείας (1864), καθόσον οὔτε τὸ Τριῴδιον σημειοῖ τοιαύτην τινὰ τάξιν, οὔτε τὸ ἀρχαῖον Τυπικὸν· ἤδη ὅμως καθιερώθη, οὕτως εἰπεῖν, καὶ ὁ λαὸς φαίνεται ποθῶν ἵνα εὐλαβῶς προσκυνήσῃ τῇ ὥρᾳ ταύτῃ τὸν διὰ τὰς ἡμετέρας ἁμαρτίας ἀναρτηθέντα ἐπὶ τοῦ ξύλου τοῦ Σταυροῦ Θεάνθρωπον.

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 3–6, Procession of the Crucified)

## Service of the Holy Passion Continued (Gospels 3–6, Procession of the Crucified)

...alternately by the Choirs at each of the Gospel readings, except the last one. The same invitation to read the Gospel is made by the Ecclesiarch to each of the subsequently officiating Hierarchs down to the last one, according to the order of seniority. After the Hierarchs, the remaining Gospels are read by the Great Protosynkellos [Grand Chancellor], the Grand Chancellor-Secretary [Archigrammateus], the Great Archimandrite, the Synkellos, and so forth in order. For these clergymen who come after the Hierarchs, however, the Deacons stand inside the Sanctuary [Bema] when they alternately intone the exclamation: **"And that we may be accounted worthy to hear the Holy Gospel..."** (*Καὶ ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι ἡμᾶς*). At the completion of the Second Gospel, the Second Choir begins chanting the Fourth Antiphon until the Kathisma in Tone 7: **"What manner of way, O Judas, led thee to become a traitor to the Savior?..."** (*Ποῖός σε τρόπος, Ἰούδα*). Then the Third Gospel [Matthew 26:57–75] is read. After the end, the Second Choir sings **"Glory to Thy forbearance, O Lord, glory to Thee!"** (*Δόξα τῇ μακροθυμίᾳ σου, Κύριε, δόξα σοι*), and the First Choir chants the Seventh Antiphon until the Kathisma in Tone 8: **"Oh, how did Judas, once Thy disciple, meditate betrayal against Thee?..."** (*Ὢ πῶς Ἰούδας ὁ ποτὲ μαθητής*), and the Fourth Gospel [John 18:28–19:16] is read. Then the Second Choir chants the Tenth Antiphon until the Kathisma in Tone 8: **"When Thou, O God, stoodest before Caiaphas, and wast delivered unto Pilate, O Judge..."** (*Ὅτε παρέστης τῷ Καϊάφᾳ, ὁ Θεός*), and the Fifth Gospel [Matthew 27:3–32] is read. After this, the First Choir begins the Thirteenth Antiphon until the Theotokion Troparion: **"Rejoice, thou who through the Angel didst receive the joy of the world!"** (*Χαῖρε ἡ δι’ Ἀγγέλου τὴν χαρὰν τοῦ κόσμου δεξαμένη*). Then the Great Archimandrite, vested in Epitrachilion and Phelonion, lifts the Crucifix [Cross with the effigy of the Crucified Christ upon it] and, circling around the Holy Table, chants in the recitative style of the Gospel with deep compunction the Idiomelon in Tone 6 [Tone 2 Plagal]: **"Today He Who suspended the earth upon the waters is suspended upon the Tree!..."** (*Σήμερον κρεμᾶται ἐπὶ ξύλου*). The Great Archimandrite exits through the North Gate of the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], preceded by the Primikirios, the candle-bearers, and two censing Deacons, and reaches the middle of the Nave, where the Cross is erected in its socket on the floor.* The First Choir repeats the same Idiomelon, chanting it in Tone 6 [Tone 2 Plagal]. Then the Second Choir chants the Stichera **"Let us not feast as the Jews, for our Passover, Christ God, is sacrificed for us..."** (*Μὴ ὡς Ἰουδαῖοι ἑορτάσωμεν*), until the Kathisma in Tone 4: **"Thou hast redeemed us from the curse of the Law by Thy precious Blood; having been nailed to the Cross and pierced with the spear, Thou hast poured forth immortality unto men: O our Savior, glory to Thee!"** (*Ἐξηγόρασας ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς κατάρας τοῦ Νόμου*), and then the Sixth Gospel [Mark 15:16–32] is read. After this, the First Choir begins the Beatitudes: **"In Thy Kingdom remember us, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom!"** (*Ἐν τῇ βασιλείᾳ σου μνήσθητι ἡμῶν, Κύριε*); and the Second Choir, arriving at the Beatitude verse: **"Blessed are they that hunger and thirst after righteousness: for they shall be filled"** (*Μακάριοι οἱ πεινῶντες καὶ διψῶντες τὴν δικαιοσύνην*, Matt. 5:6), sings the...

---

* *Violakis Historical Rubrical Note:* In the Patriarchal Church (as well as on the Holy Mountain of Athos until now) this procession of the Crucified did not take place until the patriarchate of Sophronios IV from Amasia (1864), inasmuch as neither the Triodion nor the ancient Typikon records any such order. In our time, however, it has become fully established, so to speak, since the faithful manifestly desire at this hour to venerate reverently the God-Man, Who was suspended upon the wood of the Cross for our sins.[^494]

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospels 7–11, Canon, and Praises)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν (Συνέχεια)

τροπάριον Διὰ ξύλου ὁ Ἀδάμ, καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ μετὰ τῶν ὑπολοίπων Μακαρισμῶν· μετὰ δὲ τὸ Καὶ νῦν, Τὴν Μητέρα σου Χριστέ, ψάλλεται τὸ Προκείμενον Διεμερίσαντο τὰ ἱμάτιά μου ἑαυτοῖς ἐκ γ΄ μετὰ τῶν Στίχων αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Ζ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον. Εἶτα ὁ Πατριάρχης τὸν Ν΄ ψαλμὸν χῦμα, καὶ ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Η΄ Εὐαγγέλιον. Μετὰ τοῦτο ἄρχεται ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ψάλλειν τὸν Κανόνα Πρὸς σὲ ὀρθρίζω τὸν δι’ εὐσπλαγχνίαν ἀνὰ δ΄ τροπάρια μετὰ τοῦ Δόξα σοι ὁ Θεὸς ἐν ἑκάστῃ Ὠδῇ, καὶ τὸν Εἱρμὸν ἐπαναλαμβανόμενον εἰς τὸ τέλος· μετὰ τὸν Εἱρμὸν ἡ Αἴτησις, καὶ μετὰ τὴν Ἐκφώνησιν τὸ Κοντάκιον, ὁ Οἶκος, τὸ Μηνολόγιον καὶ τὸ Ὑπόμνημα τῆς Μ. Παρασκευῆς. Μετὰ ταῦτα ἄρχεται ὁ β΄ Χορὸς τὴν η΄ Ὠδήν, μετὰ δὲ τὸν Εἱρμὸν ὁ Διάκονος ἐκφωνεῖ Τὴν Θεοτόκον καὶ μητέρα τοῦ Φωτός, καὶ ὁ Πατριάρχης ἄρχεται ψάλλειν τὴν θ΄ Ὠδὴν Τὴν τιμιωτέραν τῶν Χερουβίμ, μετὰ τὸν Εἱρμὸν ἡ Αἴτησις, καὶ ἀκολούθως ψάλλει τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον Τὸν λῃστὴν αὐθημερόν, ὅπερ ψάλλεται ἐκ γ΄· ψαλλομένου τοῦ Ἐξαποστειλαρίου ἔρχεται ὁ Πριμικήριος καὶ ἵσταται ἐν τῷ ὡρισμένῳ τόπῳ, ὡς εἴθισται· μετὰ τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Θ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον, μετ’ αὐτὸ δὲ ὁ α΄ Χορὸς ψάλλει τὸ Πᾶσα πνοή. Εἰς τὸ Αἰνεῖτε ὁ Πατριάρχης κατέρχεται τοῦ θρόνου καὶ προσκυνεῖ τὸν Ἐσταυρωμένον καὶ εὐλογήσας τὸν λαόν, τοῦ α΄ Χοροῦ ψάλλοντος τὸ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, ἀνέρχεται αὖθις καὶ ἵσταται εἰς τὸν θρόνον, οἱ δὲ Χοροὶ ψάλλουσι τὰ δ΄ τῶν Αἴνων Ἰδιόμελα· μετὰ τὸ Καὶ νῦν, Τὸν νῶτόν μου ἔδωκα εἰς μαστίγωσιν ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ Ι΄ Εὐαγγέλιον, εἶτα ὁ Πατριάρχης τὸ Σοὶ δόξα πρέπει, Κύριε, ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν χῦμα, καὶ μετὰ τὸ τέλος ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης τὸ Πληρώσωμεν τὴν Ἑωθινὴν δέησιν ἡμῶν τῷ Κυρίῳ κτλ. καὶ μετὰ τὴν Ἐκφώνησιν Σὸν γὰρ ἐστὶ τὸ ἐλεεῖν καὶ σῴζειν ἡμᾶς ἀναγινώσκεται τὸ ΙΑ΄ Εὐαγγέλιον· εἶτα ἄρχονται ψάλλεσθαι τὰ Ἀπόστιχα ἀπὸ τοῦ β΄ Χοροῦ, ὁ δὲ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος μεταξὺ τούτου ποιήσας τῷ Πατριάρχῃ σχῆμα εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερὸν καὶ ἐνδύεται τὴν στολὴν αὐτοῦ ὅπως ἀναγνώσῃ τὸ τελευταῖον Εὐαγγέλιον. Ψαλλομένου τοῦ Καὶ νῦν, Ἤδη βάπτεται κάλαμος ὑπὸ τοῦ α΄ Χοροῦ, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιδιάκονος ἐξέρχεται μετὰ τοῦ ἱεροῦ Εὐαγγελίου καὶ ἔρχεται πρὸς τὸν Πατριάρχην καὶ κύψας λέγει Εὐλόγησον Δέσποτα τὸν Εὐαγγελιστήν, ὁ Πατριάρχης ἐπιθεὶς τὴν χεῖρα, λέγει Ὁ Θεὸς διὰ πρεσβειῶν τοῦ ἁγίου ἐνδόξου Ἀποστόλου κτλ., ὁ δὲ ἐπειπὼν τρὶς τὸ Ἀμήν, εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν, καὶ ἐξελθὼν διὰ

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospels 7–11, Canon, and Praises)

## Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospels 7–11, Canon, and Praises)

...Sixth Gospel [Mark 15:16–32] is read. After the foregoing, the First Choir begins chanting the Beatitudes: **"In Thy Kingdom remember us, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom!"** (*Ἐν τῇ βασιλείᾳ σου μνήσθητι ἡμῶν, Κύριε*). The Second Choir, after chanting the Beatitude verse: **"Blessed are they that hunger and thirst after righteousness: for they shall be filled"** (*Μακάριοι οἱ πεινῶντες καὶ διψῶντες τὴν δικαιοσύνην*), sings immediately after it the Troparion: **"Through a tree Adam was estranged from Paradise, but through the tree of the Cross the thief inhabited Paradise..."** (*Διὰ ξύλου ὁ Ἀδάμ*), and the rest of the Troparia with the remaining Beatitudes. After **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), **"Thy Mother, O Christ..."** (*Τὴν Μητέρα σου, Χριστέ*), we sing the Prokeimenon in Tone 4: **"They parted My garments among them, and upon My vesture they cast lots"** (*Διεμερίσαντο τὰ ἱμάτιά μου ἑαυτοῖς...*, Ps. 21:19 LXX) three times with its verses, and the Seventh Gospel [Matthew 27:33–54] is read. The Patriarch then recites Psalm 50 [LXX] in a low voice [*χῦμα*], and the Eighth Gospel [Luke 23:32–49] is read. After this, the First Choir begins to chant the Triodion Canon [by St. Kosmas of Maiuma], Ode 5: **"Unto Thee I rise early at dawn, Who through tender compassion..."** (*Πρὸς σὲ ὀρθρίζω τὸν δι’ εὐσπλαγχνίαν*), with four Troparia at each Ode, interposing the refrain: **"Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee!"** (*Δόξα σοι, ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*) at each Ode; the Heirmos is repeated at the end [as Katavasia]. After the Heirmos, the Small Litany is intoned, and after the Exclamation, we recite the Kontakion in Tone 8: **"Come, let us all sing the praise of Him Who was crucified for us..."** (*Τὸν δι’ ἡμᾶς σταυρωθέντα...*), the Ikos, the Menologion, and the Synaxarion [Hypomnema] of Holy and Great Friday. After these, the Second Choir begins chanting the Eighth Ode. After the Heirmos of this Ode, the Deacon intones: **"The Theotokos and Mother of the Light, let us magnify in hymns honoring her!"** (*Τὴν Θεοτόκον καὶ μητέρα τοῦ Φωτός...*), and the Patriarch begins chanting the Ninth Ode with the Magnifications: **"More honorable than the Cherubim..."** (*Τὴν τιμιωτέραν τῶν Χερουβίμ*). After the Heirmos [Katavasia], the Small Litany is intoned, and following it, the Exapostilarion is chanted: **"The good thief didst Thou account worthy of Paradise in a single day, O Lord..."** (*Τὸν λῃστὴν αὐθημερόν*), which is sung three times. While the Exapostilarion is being chanted, the Primikirios comes and stands in his appointed place, as is customary. After the Exapostilarion, the Ninth Gospel [John 19:25–37] is read. After this, the First Choir chants: **"Let every breath praise the Lord!"** (*Πᾶσα πνοὴ αἰνεσάτω τὸν Κύριον*). At the verse: **"Praise Him, all His angels..."** (*Αἰνεῖτε αὐτόν, πάντες οἱ ἄγγελοι αὐτοῦ*), the Patriarch descends from the Throne, bows and venerates the Crucifix [Cross with the effigy of the Crucified Christ], and blesses the people while the First Choir chants **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*). He ascends again and stands upon the Throne, and the Choirs chant the four Idiomela of the Praises (*Ainoi*). After **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), the Doxastikon in Tone 2: **"I gave My back to scourgings, and My cheeks to blows..."** (*Τὸν νῶτόν μου ἔδωκα εἰς μαστίγωσιν*), the Tenth Gospel [Mark 15:43–47] is read. The Patriarch then recites the Small Doxology: **"Glory to God in the highest..."** and **"To Thee glory is due, O Lord our God..."** (*Σοὶ δόξα πρέπει, Κύριε, ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*) in a low voice [*χῦμα*]; and at the end the Great Archimandrite intones: **"Let us complete our morning prayer unto the Lord..."** (*Πληρώσωμεν τὴν ἑωθινὴν δέησιν ἡμῶν τῷ Κυρίῳ*), etc. After the Exclamation: **"For Thine it is to show mercy and to save us, O our God, and unto Thee do we send up glory, to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Σὸν γὰρ ἐστὶ τὸ ἐλεεῖν καὶ σῴζειν ἡμᾶς*), the Eleventh Gospel [John 19:38–42] is read. Then the Aposticha begin to be chanted by the Second Choir. In the meantime, the Great Archdeacon, having made a reverent sign (*σχῆμα*) to the Patriarch, enters the Holy Sanctuary [Bema] and vests in his vestment so that he may proclaim the final Gospel. As the First Choir chants the Doxastikon at **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), Tone 8: **"Already the pen of sentence is dipped in ink by unjust judges..."** (*Ἤδη βάπτεται κάλαμος ἀποφάσεως...*), the Great Archdeacon exits the Holy Sanctuary with the Holy Gospel Book and comes before the Patriarch. Bowing his...[^495]

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospel 12, Dismissal, and Departure)

## Ἡ ἐν τῷ Πατριαρχικῷ Ναῷ Τάξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἁγίων Παθῶν (Τέλος)

τῆς Βορείου πύλης ἀνέρχεται εἰς τὸν Ἄμβωνα. Μετὰ τὸ τέλος τοῦ Ἰδιομέλου, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης ἱστάμενος εἰς τὴν Ὡραίαν πύλην ἐκφωνεῖ Σοφία, ὀρθοί, ἀκούσωμεν τοῦ ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου, ὁ Πατριάρχης Εἰρήνη πᾶσι, οἱ Χοροὶ Καὶ τῷ πνεύματί σου, ὁ Ἀρχιδιάκονος ἀπὸ τοῦ Ἄμβωνος Ἐκ τοῦ κατὰ Ματθαῖον ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου, καὶ ἀναγινώσκει τὸ ΙΒ΄ καὶ τελευταῖον Εὐαγγέλιον Τῇ ἐπαύριον ἥτις ἐστὶ μετὰ τὴν Παρασκευήν· μετὰ τὸ τέλος, ὁ Πατριάρχης εὐλογεῖ, οἱ δὲ Χοροὶ ψάλλουσι τὸ Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, ὁ Ἀρχιδιάκονος κατελθὼν ἀπὸ τοῦ Ἄμβωνος φέρει τὸ ἱερὸν Εὐαγγέλιον τῷ Πατριάρχῃ πρὸς ἀσπασμόν, καὶ ἀσπασάμενος τὴν χεῖρα τοῦ Πατριάρχου εἰσέρχεται εἰς τὸ Ἱερόν. Μετὰ ταῦτα ὁ Πατριάρχης λέγει τὸ Ἀγαθὸν τὸ ἐξομολογεῖσθαι τῷ Κυρίῳ, ὁ δὲ Ἀναγνώστης Ἱερεὺς τὸ Τρισάγιον, καὶ ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία· τότε οἱ Χοροὶ ψάλλουσιν ἐκ τρίτου τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τροπάριον Ἐξηγόρασας ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς κατάρας τοῦ νόμου τῷ τιμίῳ σου αἵματι· εἶτα ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης ἔσωθεν, Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός, κατὰ τὸ μέγα ἔλεός σου . . ἔτι δεόμεθα ὑπὲρ τῶν εὐσεβῶν καὶ ὀρθοδόξων Χριστιανῶν, ἔτι δεόμεθα ὑπὲρ τοῦ Ἀρχιεπισκόπου ἡμῶν . . Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος Θεὸς ὑπάρχεις· ὁ Ἱεροδιάκονος Σοφία, ὁ Ἀρχιμανδρίτης Ὁ ὢν εὐλογητὸς Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ὁ Πατριάρχης Στερεώσαι Κύριος ὁ Θεός, ὁ Μ. Ἀρχιμανδρίτης Δόξα σοι, Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι, ὁ δι’ ὑπερβάλλουσαν ἀγαθότητα κτλ. Ἐν ᾧ λέγεται ἡ Ἀπόλυσις,) ὁ Πατριάρχης εὐλογῶν τὸν λαὸν ἀπὸ τοῦ θρόνου, τῶν Χορῶν ψαλλόντων Τὸν δεσπότην καὶ Ἀρχιερέα ἡμῶν, κατέρχεται τοῦ θρόνου, καὶ ποιήσας τρὶς τὸ σημεῖον τοῦ Σταυροῦ ἐξέρχεται τοῦ Ναοῦ μετὰ τῆς αὐτῆς παρατάξεως, τοῦ Ἱερέως ἐκφωνοῦντος ἀπὸ τοῦ Βήματος Δι’ εὐχῶν τοῦ ἁγίου Δεσπότου ἡμῶν, φθάσας δὲ εἰς τὸν Νάρθηκα ἀποδύεται τὸν Μανδύαν καὶ ἀνερχόμενος τὰς βαθμίδας τοῦ Νάρθηκος, στραφεὶς εὐλογεῖ, τοῦ Μ. Πριμικηρίου ἐκφωνοῦντος τὸν Πολυχρονισμὸν αὐτοῦ2, ἀνελθὼν δὲ τὴν μεγάλην τῆς Πρωτοσυγκελλίας κλίμακα ἵσταται εἰς τὸν ἐξώστην καὶ εὐλογήσας αὖθις τὸν ἐν τῷ περιβόλῳ τοῦ Ναοῦ ἱστάμενον λαὸν ἀναμένοντα καὶ τελευταῖον τὴν πατριαρχικὴν

----------
2. Πολυχρόνιον ποιήσαι Κύριος ὁ Θεὸς τὸν παναγιώτατον καὶ σεβασμιώτατον ἡμῶν αὐθέντην καὶ Δεσπότην τὸν Οἰκουμενικὸν Πατριάρχην κύριον κύριον [δεῖνα]· Κύριε φύλαττε αὐτὸν εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη· Ἀμήν.

# Appendix: Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospel 12, Dismissal, and Departure)

## Service of the Holy Passion Concluded (Gospel 12, Dismissal, and Departure)

...head, he says: **"Bless, Master, the proclaimer of the Gospel."** (*Εὐλόγησον, Δέσποτα, τὸν εὐαγγελιστήν*). Placing his hand on the head of the Great Archdeacon, the Patriarch says: **"May God, through the intercessions of the holy, glorious, and all-laudable Apostle and Evangelist Matthew, grant unto thee who proclaimest the Gospel..."** (*Ὁ Θεὸς διὰ πρεσβειῶν τοῦ ἁγίου ἐνδόξου Ἀποστόλου...*), etc. The Great Archdeacon, repeating **"Amen"** three times, enters again into the Holy Sanctuary [Bema], and, exiting from it through the North Gate, ascends the Ambo. At the conclusion of the Idiomelon [**"Already the pen of sentence is dipped in ink..."**], the Great Archimandrite, standing in the Royal Doors [Beautiful Gate], exclaims: **"Wisdom! Stand upright! Let us hear the Holy Gospel!"** (*Σοφία, ὀρθοί, ἀκούσωμεν τοῦ ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου*). The Patriarch: **"Peace be unto all."** (*Εἰρήνη πᾶσι*). The Choirs: **"And with thy spirit."** (*Καὶ τῷ πνεύματί σου*). The Archdeacon from the Ambo: **"The reading is from the Holy Gospel according to Matthew"** (*Ἐκ τοῦ κατὰ Ματθαῖον ἁγίου Εὐαγγελίου*); he then proclaims the Twelfth and final Gospel: **"Now the next day, that followed the day of the preparation..."** (*Τῇ ἐπαύριον ἥτις ἐστὶ μετὰ τὴν Παρασκευήν*, Matt. 27:62–66). At the end of the reading, the Patriarch blesses the proclaimer and the people, while the Choirs chant **"For many years, Master!"** (*Εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη, Δέσποτα*). Descending from the Ambo, the Archdeacon brings the Holy Gospel Book to the Patriarch to venerate by kissing it. The Archdeacon, having kissed the Patriarch’s hand, enters the Holy Sanctuary. After this, the Patriarch says: **"It is a good thing to give praise unto the Lord, and to sing unto Thy name, O Most High..."** (*Ἀγαθὸν τὸ ἐξομολογεῖσθαι τῷ Κυρίῳ...*, Ps. 91:2 LXX). The Reader-Priest recites the Trisagion Prayers, and the Great Archimandrite exclaims: **"For Thine is the kingdom, and the power, and the glory, of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία*). Then the Choirs chant the Dismissal Troparion in Tone 4: **"Thou hast redeemed us from the curse of the Law by Thy precious Blood; having been nailed to the Cross and pierced with the spear, Thou hast poured forth immortality unto men: O our Savior, glory to Thee!"** (*Ἐξηγόρασας ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς κατάρας τοῦ νόμου τῷ τιμίῳ σου αἵματι...*) three times. Then the Great Archimandrite from inside the Holy Sanctuary intones: **"Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy, we pray Thee, hear and have mercy..."** (*Ἐλέησον ἡμᾶς ὁ Θεός...*), **"Again we pray for all pious and Orthodox Christians..."**, **"Again we pray for our Archbishop [Ecumenical Patriarch N.]..."**, and the Exclamation: **"For Thou art a merciful God and lovest mankind, and unto Thee do we send up glory, to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὅτι ἐλεήμων καὶ φιλάνθρωπος Θεὸς ὑπάρχεις*). The Hierodeacon: **"Wisdom!"** (*Σοφία*). The Archimandrite: **"Blessed is He Who is, Christ our God, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὁ ὢν εὐλογητὸς Θεὸς ἡμῶν*). The Patriarch: **"Establish, O Lord God, the holy Orthodox faith of Orthodox Christians unto ages of ages!"** (*Στερεώσαι Κύριος ὁ Θεός...*). The Great Archimandrite: **"Glory to Thee, O Christ God, our hope, glory to Thee!"** (*Δόξα σοι, Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἡ ἐλπὶς ἡμῶν, δόξα σοι*), and intones the Dismissal: **"He Who endured spitting and scourgings, buffetings and the Cross and death, for our salvation, Christ our true God, through the intercessions of His all-immaculate and blameless holy Mother..."** (*Ὁ δι’ ὑπερβάλλουσαν ἀγαθότητα...*). While the Dismissal is being spoken, the Patriarch blesses the people from his Throne, as the Choirs sing: **"Our Master and Chief Priest, O Lord, preserve and keep for many years!"** (*Τὸν δεσπότην καὶ Ἀρχιερέα ἡμῶν, Κύριε, φύλαττε εἰς πολλὰ ἔτη*). The Patriarch then descends from his Throne and, having signed himself three times with the sign of the Cross, exits the church with his entourage, the Priest from the Holy Sanctuary exclaiming: **"Through the prayers of our holy Master, Lord Jesus Christ our God, have mercy on us. Amen."** (*Δι’ εὐχῶν τοῦ ἁγίου Δεσπότου ἡμῶν...*). Having arrived at the Narthex, the Patriarch takes off his Mandyas [Hierarchical Mantle] and, ascending the stairs of the Narthex, he turns and blesses, while the Great Primikirios intones his Polychronion [Solemn Acclamation].* Having ascended the great stairs of the Chancellory [Protosynkellia], the Patriarch stands on the balcony and...

---

* *Patriarchal Polychronion:* "May the Lord God grant many years unto our most holy and venerable Master, the Ecumenical Patriarch [N.]! O Lord, preserve him for many years! Amen."[^496]

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 1 and 2)

## Διάταξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας Ἐγκαινίων Ναοῦ

Εὐλογίαν, εἰσέρχεται διὰ τοῦ διαδρόμου τῆς Πρωτοσυγκελλίας εἰς τὰ πατριαρχικὰ δωμάτια.

ΔΙΑΤΑΞΙΣ ΤΗΣ ΑΚΟΛΟΥΘΙΑΣ
ΕΓΚΑΙΝΙΩΝ ΝΑΟΥ
----------------------------------------

Α΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν Ἐγκαίνια Ναοῦ ἐν μνήμῃ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ εἰς τὸ Κύριε ἐκέκραξα ψάλλονται Στιχηρὰ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων δ΄, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου γ΄, καὶ γ΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ ἐγκαινιαζομένου Ναοῦ, Δόξα τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, Ἀναγνώσματα β΄ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων καὶ ἓν τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τὰ Ἀπόστιχα τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τὸ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.

Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον, τὰ Καθίσματα τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων ἀντὶ Θεοτοκίων, ὁ Κανὼν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ ἀνὰ δ΄, τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων β΄ τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου γ΄, καὶ γ΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ (ἐὰν μὴ ᾖ ἑορτὴ τῶν Ἀποστόλων, ἢ τοῦ Προδρόμου, ἢ τῶν Εὐαγγελιστῶν, ἤ τινος τῶν ἐπισήμως ἑορταζομένων Ἁγίων), Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.

Β΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν ἐν Κυριακῇ, εἰς τὸ Κύριε ἐκέκραξα ψάλλονται Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων γ΄, καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ γ΄, Δόξα Ἀναγνώσματα τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Ἀπόστιχα,

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 1 and 2)

## Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 1 and 2)

...bestows one final patriarchal blessing upon the waiting people standing in the church courtyard. He then enters the Patriarchal apartments [residence] through the corridor of the Chancellory [Protosynkellia].

# ORDER OF SERVICE FOR THE CONSECRATION OF A CHURCH

## (§ Appendix: Consecration of a Temple — Order of Rubrics)

---

### Case 1: If the Consecration of a Church Falls on the Commemoration of a Celebrated Saint

If the Consecration of a Church takes place on the commemoration of a celebrated Saint, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 10 Stichera are chanted: 4 of the Consecration, 3 of the celebrated Saint, and 3 of the Patron Saint of the church being consecrated; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the celebrated Saint; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Old Testament Readings: two of the Consecration and one of the celebrated Saint. At the Aposticha: Stichera of the celebrated Saint; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Then the Dismissal Troparia: that of the celebrated Saint, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration.

At Matins: the Kathismata of the celebrated Saint, and those of the Consecration instead of Theotokia. Canons: the Canon of the Consecration, that of the celebrated Saint, and that of the Patron Saint of the church, each Ode with 4 Troparia [totaling 12]. Exapostilaria: that of the celebrated Saint, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 2 of the Consecration, 3 of the celebrated Saint, and 3 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church (unless it be the feast of the Apostles, or of the Forerunner, or of the Evangelists, or of any solemnly celebrated saint, in which case the Doxastikon is of the celebrated Saint); **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration; the Great Doxology, and the Dismissal Troparion of the Consecration.

### Case 2: If the Consecration Takes Place on a Sunday

If the Consecration takes place on a Sunday, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 10 Stichera are chanted: 4 of the Resurrection, 3 of the Consecration...[^497]

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 2 Concluded, 3, and 4)

## Διάταξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας Ἐγκαινίων Ναοῦ (Συνέχεια)

Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἀπολυτίκιον, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.

Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Καθίσματα μετὰ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων ἀντὶ Θεοτοκίων, ὁ ἀναστάσιμος Κανών, ὁ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, καὶ ὁ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἐξαποστειλάριον, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους, Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων δ΄, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Σήμερον σωτηρία.

Γ΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν ἐν Κυριακῇ μετὰ μνήμης ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ Ἀναστάσιμα γ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων β΄, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου γ΄ καὶ β΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Δόξα τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, Καὶ νῦν τὸ α΄ Θεοτοκίον τοῦ ἤχου, Ἀναγνώσματα β΄ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων καὶ ἓν τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Ἀπόστιχα, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἀπολυτίκιον, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.
Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Καθίσματα, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων ἀντὶ τῶν Θεοτοκίων, ὁ ἀναστάσιμος Κανών, ὁ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, καὶ ὁ τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἐξαποστειλάριον, τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους, Ἀναστάσιμα γ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων β΄, καὶ γ΄ τοῦ ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ (ἐὰν μὴ ᾖ, ὡς προείρηται, ἡ μνήμη ἐπισήμως ἑορταζομένου Ἁγίου), Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Σήμερον σωτηρία.

Δ΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν ἐν Θεομητορικῇ ἑορτῇ, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ ψάλλονται Στιχηρὰ τῆς ἑορτῆς δ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων γ΄, καὶ γ΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 2 Concluded, 3, and 4)

## Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 2 Concluded, 3, and 4)

...tion and 3 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), the first Dogmatic Theotokion of the Tone of the week. The Readings of the Consecration. At the Aposticha: Resurrection Stichera; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Dismissal Troparia: the Sunday Resurrection Troparion, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration.

At Matins: the Resurrection Kathismata, and those of the Consecration in place of the Theotokia. Canons: the Resurrection Canon, that of the Consecration, and that of the Patron Saint of the church. Exapostilaria: the Sunday Resurrection Exapostilarion, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 4 of the Resurrection and 4 of the Consecration; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Great Doxology, and the Sunday Troparion: **"Today salvation is come unto the world..."** (*Σήμερον σωτηρία τῷ κόσμῳ γέγονεν*).

### Case 3: If the Consecration Takes Place on a Sunday with the Commemoration of a Celebrated Saint

If the Consecration takes place on a Sunday with the commemoration of a celebrated Saint, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 10 Stichera are chanted: 3 of the Resurrection, 2 of the Consecration, 3 of the celebrated Saint, and 2 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the celebrated Saint; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), the first Dogmatic Theotokion of the Tone of the week. Two Readings of the Consecration and one of the celebrated Saint. At the Aposticha: Resurrection Stichera; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Dismissal Troparia: the Sunday Resurrection Troparion, that of the celebrated Saint, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration.

At Matins: the Resurrection Kathismata, those of the celebrated Saint, and those of the Consecration in place of the Theotokia. Canons: the Resurrection Canon, that of the Consecration, and that of the celebrated Saint. Exapostilaria: the Sunday Resurrection Exapostilarion, that of the celebrated Saint, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 3 of the Resurrection, 2 of the Consecration, and 3 of the celebrated Saint; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church (unless, as has been said, it be the feast of the Apostles, or of the Forerunner, or of the Evangelists, or of a solemnly celebrated saint); **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Great Doxology, and the Sunday Troparion: **"Today salvation is come unto the world..."** (*Σήμερον σωτηρία...*).

### Case 4: If the Consecration Takes Place on a Feast of the Mother of God (Theotokos)

If the Consecration takes place on a Feast of the Mother of God, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 10 Stichera are chanted: 4 of the Feast, 3 of the Consecration, and 3 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration...[^498]

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 4 Concluded, 5, and 6)

## Διάταξις τῆς Ἀκολουθίας Ἐγκαινίων Ναοῦ (Συνέχεια)

Ἀναγνώσματα β΄ τῆς ἑορτῆς καὶ ἓν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὰ Ἀπόστιχα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῆς ἑορτῆς, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς.
Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον καὶ εἰς τὰς γ΄ Στιχολογίας, τὸ Κάθισμα τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς, οἱ Κανόνες τῆς ἑορτῆς καὶ ὁ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τοῦ Ἁγίου καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους τῆς ἑορτῆς δ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων β΄, καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου β΄. Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς.

Ε΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν ἐν Δεσποτικῇ ἑορτῇ, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ ψάλλονται τῆς ἑορτῆς ς΄ καὶ β΄ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, Ἀναγνώσματα τῆς ἑορτῆς β΄ καὶ ἓν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὰ Ἀπόστιχα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῆς ἑορτῆς, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς.

Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον καὶ εἰς τὰς γ΄ Στιχολογίας, τὸ Κάθισμα τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς, οἱ Κανόνες τῆς ἑορτῆς, καὶ ὁ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ Ἐξαποστειλάριον τῆς ἑορτῆς, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ αὖθις τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους, τῆς ἑορτῆς δ΄, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων β΄ καὶ τοῦ Ἁγίου β΄, Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τῆς ἑορτῆς.

ΣΤ΄.

Ἂν τύχωσιν ἐν Κυριακῇ μετὰ Θεομητορικῆς ἑορτῆς, ἐν τῷ Ἑσπερινῷ ψάλλονται Ἀναστάσιμα δ΄, τῆς ἑορτῆς δ΄, καὶ β΄ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, Ἀναγνώσματα β΄ τῆς ἑορτῆς, καὶ ἓν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Ἀπόστιχα, Δόξα τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, Καὶ νῦν τῆς ἑορτῆς, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἀπολυτίκιον, τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων.

Εἰς τὸν Ὄρθρον, τὰ ἀναστάσιμα Καθίσματα, τῶν Ἐγκαινίων καὶ τὰ τῆς ἑορτῆς, ὁ ἀναστάσιμος Κανών, ὁ τῆς ἑορτῆς καὶ ὁ τῶν

# Appendix: Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 4 Concluded, 5, and 6)

## Order of Service for the Consecration of a Church (Situations 4 Concluded, 5, and 6)

...of the Consecration. Two Readings of the Feast and one of the Consecration. At the Aposticha: Stichera of the Feast; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Feast. Dismissal Troparia: the Troparion of the Feast, that of the Consecration, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and again that of the Feast.

At Matins, and in the three poetic Kathisma seatings [Stichologies]: the Kathisma of the Feast, that of the Consecration, and again that of the Feast. Canons: the Canons of the Feast and that of the Consecration. Exapostilaria: of the Feast, that of the Consecration, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and again that of the Feast. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 4 of the Feast, 2 of the Consecration, and 2 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Great Doxology, and the Dismissal Troparion of the Feast.

### Case 5: If the Consecration Takes Place on a Feast of the Lord (Despotic Feast)

If the Consecration takes place on a Feast of the Lord, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 8 Stichera are chanted: 6 of the Feast and 2 of the Consecration; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Two Readings of the Feast and one of the Consecration. At the Aposticha: Stichera of the Feast; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Feast. Dismissal Troparia: the Troparion of the Feast, that of the Consecration, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and again that of the Feast.

At Matins, and in the three poetic Kathisma seatings [Stichologies]: the Kathisma of the Feast, that of the Consecration, and again that of the Feast. Canons: the Canons of the Feast and that of the Consecration. Exapostilaria: of the Feast, that of the Consecration, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and again that of the Feast. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 4 of the Feast, 2 of the Consecration, and 2 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Great Doxology, and the Dismissal Troparion of the Feast.

### Case 6: If the Consecration Takes Place on a Sunday with a Feast of the Mother of God (Theotokos)

If the Consecration takes place on a Sunday with a Feast of the Theotokos, at Vespers, at **"Lord, I have cried"** (*Κύριε, ἐκέκραξα*), 10 Stichera are chanted: 4 of the Resurrection, 4 of the Feast, and 2 of the Consecration; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Two Readings of the Feast and one of the Consecration. At the Aposticha: Resurrection Stichera; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Feast. Dismissal Troparia: the Sunday Resurrection Troparion, that of the Feast, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration.

At Matins: the Resurrection Kathismata, those of the Consecration...[^499]

# Appendix: The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Preparations and Pre-Vesperal Rites)

## Ἡ Τελετὴ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων (Προπαρασκευαὶ καὶ Προερτόδιοι Τάξεις)

Ἐγκαινίων, τὸ ἀναστάσιμον Ἐξαποστειλάριον, τὸ τῆς ἑορτῆς, τοῦ Ἁγίου τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων. Εἰς τοὺς Αἴνους, Ἀναστάσιμα γ΄, τῆς ἑορτῆς γ΄, καὶ β΄ τοῦ Ἁγίου, Δόξα τῆς ἑορτῆς, Καὶ νῦν τῶν Ἐγκαινίων, ἡ Μεγάλη Δοξολογία καὶ τὸ Σήμερον σωτηρία.

Ἀπόστολος καὶ Εὐαγγέλιον ἐν ἑκάστῃ τῶν προταχθεισῶν περιπτώσεων ὁρίζονται ὑπὸ τοῦ Τελετουργοῦ ἀναλόγως τῆς τυχούσης ἑορτῆς.

### Η ΤΕΛΕΤΗ ΤΩΝ ΕΓΚΑΙΝΙΩΝ

Ὀφείλει ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ἔχειν προητοιμασμένα τὰ ἀπαιτούμενα εἰς τὴν καθιέρωσιν καὶ τὸν Ἐγκαινιασμὸν τοῦ Ναοῦ διάφορα εἴδη ὡς ἐν τῷ Εὐχολογίῳ σημειοῦνται· καὶ ἀφ’ ἑσπέρας ἀπέρχεται μετὰ τοῦ Κλήρου εἰς τὸν νέον Ναὸν καὶ καταρτίζει πρὸ τοῦ Ἑσπερινοῦ τὰ ἅγια Λείψανα εἰς μερίδας τρεῖς ἐπὶ τῆς ἁγίας Τραπέζης· καὶ τεθέντων τούτων ἔνδον τοῦ Δισκαρίου καὶ ἄνωθεν ἀστερίσκου καὶ καλυφθέντων μετὰ καλύμματος, ποιεῖ Εὐλογητόν, τὸ Τρισάγιον, Ὅτι σοῦ, καὶ ψάλλονται τὰ Τροπάρια Οἱ μάρτυρές σου Κύριε, Ἀθλοφόροι Κυρίου, τοὺς Μάρτυρας Χριστοῦ, τὸ τῆς ἡμέρας Τροπάριον, εἶτα τὸ Ὡς ἀπ’ ἀρχὰς τῆς φύσεως, Δόξα, Μαρτύρων θεῖος χορός, Καὶ νῦν, Θεοτόκε σὺ εἶ ἡ ἄμπελος, μετὰ τῶν Ἀθλοφόρων, καὶ Ἀπόλυσις. Καὶ εἰ μέν ἐστι πλησίον ἄλλος Ναὸς ἐγκαινιασμένος, μεταφέρει ταῦτα ἐν αὐτῷ πρὸ τοῦ Ἑσπερινοῦ, καὶ τίθησιν ἐπὶ τῆς ἁγίας Τραπέζης, ἔνθα καὶ καίει κηρὸς δι’ ὅλης τῆς νυκτός· ψάλλεται δὲ Λυχνικὸν καὶ παννυχὶς ἐν τῷ Ναῷ τῷ ἀποδεξαμένῳ τὰ Λείψανα τῶν Ἁγίων· εἰ δὲ οὐκ ἔστι τοιοῦτος Ναός, πᾶσα ἡ Ἀκολουθία γίνεται ἐν τῷ νέῳ Ναῷ, ὡς ἡ καθωρισμένη τάξις.

Τὸ πρωῒ ψάλλεται ὁ Ὄρθρος, κατὰ τὴν τυπικὴν τῆς τυχούσης ἡμέρας διάταξιν (ὅρα τὰς διατάξεις § α΄-ς΄). Μετὰ δὲ τὸ Πᾶσα πνοὴ καὶ τὸ Αἰνεῖτε, κατέρχεται ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ἀπὸ τοῦ θρόνου εἰς τὸ μέσον τοῦ Ναοῦ, καὶ κλίνας τὰ γόνατα, βλέπων πρὸς Ἀνατολὰς ἀναγινώσκει τὰς δύο προκαταρκτικὰς εὐχὰς τῶν...

# Appendix: The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Preparations and Pre-Vesperal Rites)

## The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Preliminary Preparations and Pre-Vesperal Rites)

...and those of the Feast. The Resurrection Canon, that of the Feast, and that of the Consecration. Exapostilaria: the Sunday Resurrection Exapostilarion, that of the Feast, that of the Patron Saint of the church, and that of the Consecration. At the Praises (*Ainoi*): 8 Stichera: 3 of the Resurrection, 3 of the Feast, and 2 of the Patron Saint of the church; **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*) of the Feast; **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*) of the Consecration. Great Doxology, and the Sunday Dismissal Troparion: **"Today salvation is come unto the world..."** (*Σήμερον σωτηρία τῷ κόσμῳ γέγονεν*).

The Apostolos and Gospel readings for each of the above situations are decided and appointed by the Chief Celebrant [presiding Bishop] appropriately for the particular feast at hand.

### THE CEREMONY OF THE CONSECRATION

The [presiding] Bishop must have prepared in advance all the various items, as prescribed in the Great Euchologion (*Sluzhebnik / Trebnik*), that are required for the dedication and Consecration. On the eve of the service, the Bishop proceeds with the Clergy to the new church and, before Vespers, arranges the Holy Relics in three portions upon the Holy Table. When the Holy Relics have been placed on the Diskarion [Paten] and covered with a Veil over the Asterisk [star], the Bishop intones: **"Blessed is our God, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*). This is followed by the Trisagion Prayers and the Exclamation **"For Thine is the kingdom..."** (*Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν ἡ βασιλεία*), and the following Troparia are chanted: **"Thy Martyrs, O Lord, in their sufferings..."** (*Οἱ μάρτυρές σου, Κύριε*), **"Champions of the Lord..."** (*Ἀθλοφόροι Κυρίου*), **"Let us all implore Christ's Martyrs..."** (*Τοὺς Μάρτυρας Χριστοῦ*), and the Troparion of the day. Then, the Kontakion: **"As first fruits of nature..."** (*Ὡς ἀπαρχὰς τῆς φύσεως*); **"Glory..."** (*Δόξα*), **"Godlike choir of Martyrs..."** (*Μαρτύρων θεῖος χορός*); **"Now and forever..."** (*Καὶ νῦν*), **"Theotokos, thou art the true vine, together with the Champions..."** (*Θεοτόκε, σὺ εἶ ἡ ἄμπελος*), and the Dismissal. If there is another consecrated church nearby, the Bishop transfers the Holy Relics to it before Vespers and places them on the Holy Table, where a candle burns through the whole night. Vespers and an All-night Vigil are chanted in the church that has received the Relics of the Saints.* If, however, there is no nearby consecrated church, the whole Office takes place in the new church, in accordance with the prescribed order.

In the morning, Matins is chanted according to the typikon order of service of the occurring day (see the detailed orders in Cases 1–6 above). After **"Let every breath..."** (*Πᾶσα πνοή*) and **"Praise the Lord..."** (*Αἰνεῖτε*), the Bishop descends...[^500]

---

* *Note on Vigil:* Even though according to the ancient order on the occasion of the Consecration of a Church an all-night Vigil was prescribed, currently only Vespers is chanted at its customary hour, and in the morning Matins, after which the Consecration is celebrated, followed by the Divine Liturgy.

# Appendix: The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Vesting, Wax-Mastic, and Procession)

## Ἡ Τελετὴ τῶν Ἐγκαινίων (Συνέχεια: Ἀμφίεσις, Κηρομαστίχη, καὶ Λιτανεία)

Ἐγκαινίων Καλλίστου τοῦ ἁγιωτάτου Πατριάρχου, τὰς πρὸ τῆς Ἀκολουθίας τῶν Ἐγκαινίων ἐν τῷ Εὐχολογίῳ κειμένας, εἶτα ἐγειρόμενος προσκυνεῖ τὰς ἁγίας Εἰκόνας καὶ προετοιμάζεται ὡς συνήθως διὰ τὴν Λειτουργίαν, ψαλλομένου τοῦ Τὸν δεσπότην καὶ Ἀρχιερέα, μεθ’ ὃ ψάλλονται τὰ Στιχηρὰ τῶν Αἴνων, τὸ Δοξαστικόν, ἡ Δοξολογία, τὸ Ἀπολυτίκιον τοῦ Ὄρθρου καὶ ἡ Ἀπόλυσις.

Εἶτα τὰ τῆς Κηρομαστίχης εἴδη τῇ χύτρᾳ ἐγχέονται καὶ τίθεται αὕτη ἐπὶ τοῦ πυρός, μετὰ δὲ τὸ διαλυθῆναι αὐτά, πλησίον πάλιν τὴν χύτραν κατέχουσι τοῦ πυρὸς ἵνα μὴ ἀποψυχρανθῶσι τὰ ἐν αὐτῇ· ὡσαύτως καὶ ἑτέρα χύτρα πληροῦται ὕδατος καὶ παρατίθεται τῷ πυρί, ὅπως θερμανθῇ, εἰς ἔκπλυσιν τῆς ἁγίας Τραπέζης· εἶτα περιδεσμοῦνται τὰ χαρτία εἰς τὸ χεῖλος τοῦ Κίονος, ὥστε ἐξέχειν αὐτὰ ἐπάνω τοῦ χείλους δακτύλους δύο, καὶ ἀναγεμίζεται ἔσωθεν ὁ γῦρος τῶν χαρτίων μὲ τετριμμένον μάρμαρον ἵνα μὴ ῥεύσῃ ἡ Κηρομαστίχη.

Καὶ πάντων τῶν ἄλλων χρησίμων προετοιμασθέντων, ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ποιεῖ Εὐλογητόν, καὶ εὐθὺς τὸ Κύριε εἰσάκουσον τῆς προσευχῆς μου, εἶτα Συναπτὴ μικρὰ παρὰ τοῦ Διακόνου Ἔτι καὶ ἔτι, καὶ ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς ἐκφωνεῖ Ὅτι Ἅγιος εἶ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ὁ Διάκονος Τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν, ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς τὴν Εὐχήν, εἶτα Εἰρήνη πᾶσι, ὁ Διάκονος Τὰς κεφαλὰς ἡμῶν, ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς τὴν Εὐχὴν μυστικῶς καὶ τὴν Ἐκφώνησιν Εἴη τὸ κράτος.

Καὶ μετὰ τὴν ἐκφώνησιν, [ὁ Ἀρχιερεὺς] αἴρει τὰ ἅγια Λείψανα ἐπὶ τῆς κεφαλῆς μετὰ ἁγίου Δίσκου, προπορευομένων τῶν Ἱερέων μετὰ Εὐαγγελίων, Σταυροῦ τε καὶ Ἑξαπτερύγων καὶ Λαμπάδων, τῶν δὲ Ψαλτῶν ψαλλόντων τὰ Τροπάρια Ἅγιοι Μάρτυρες, Δόξα σοι Χριστὲ ὁ Θεός, ἐξέρχεται τοῦ Ναοῦ (ὡς καὶ ἅπας ὁ λαός) καὶ λιτανεύει περὶ αὐτὸν κύκλῳ, ψαλλομένων τῶν Ἰδιομέλων Ἐγκαινίζου, ἐγκαινίζου· Πάλαι μὲν ἐγκαινίζων...

# Appendix: The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Vesting, Wax-Mastic, and Procession)

## The Ceremony of the Consecration of a Church (Vesting, Wax-Mastic Preparation, and Procession)

...down from his Throne into the middle of the church and, having knelt down facing East, he reads the two preparatory prayers of the Consecration by the most holy Patriarch Kallistos, which are found in the Euchologion (*Sluzhebnik / Trebnik*). He then rises and venerates the holy Icons and prepares as usual for the Divine Liturgy while **"Our Master and Chief Priest..."** (*Τὸν δεσπότην καὶ Ἀρχιερέα*) is chanted, followed by the Stichira of the Praises (*Ainoi*), the Doxastikon, the Great Doxology, the Dismissal Troparion of Matins, and the Dismissal.

Then the various ingredients of the wax-mastic (*κηρομαστίχη*) are poured into a vessel [cauldron] which is then placed on the fire. When they have melted, the vessel is kept close to the fire so that what it contains does not get cold and congeal. Likewise, other vessels are filled with water and put on the fire, so that they are heated for the washing of the Holy Table. Heavy paper strips are then tied around the lips of the central Column,* so that they extend about two finger-breadths [an inch] above the lip. The ring of papers is filled inside with crushed marble, so that the wax-mastic does not run over the edge.

When everything else needed is ready, the Bishop gives the blessing: **"Blessed is our God, always, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Εὐλογητὸς ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*), and at once Psalm 142 [LXX] is recited: **"O Lord, hear my prayer..."** (*Κύριε, εἰσάκουσον τῆς προσευχῆς μου*), followed by the Small Litany intoned by the Deacon: **"Again and again in peace let us pray unto the Lord"** (*Ἔτι καὶ ἔτι ἐν εἰρήνῃ τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν*). The Bishop then exclaims: **"For holy art Thou, our God, and unto Thee do we send up glory, to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and forever, and unto the ages of ages. Amen."** (*Ὅτι Ἅγιος εἶ, ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν*). Then the Deacon: **"Let us pray to the Lord"** (*Τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν*); then the Bishop recites the Prayer and intones: **"Peace be unto all"** (*Εἰρήνη πᾶσι*).
